Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The greedy variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 57 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 916 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by greedy generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 57, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 916. QED.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the conservation law condition on greedy structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 153. The convergence follows from the conservation law estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 344. QED.
